The kind of keyword that lived in the purgatory between \"worth fighting for\" and \"why bother.\"\n\nHe exported the SERP. I can get forty referring domains.*\n\nHe pulled the long-tails next. *email automation for marketing agencies comparison*, *activecampaign vs klaviyo for agency use*, *customer.io agency pricing*, *best email marketing software for agency clients*, *email automation agency white label*, *mailchimp alternative for agencies 2024*, *sendgrid vs postmark for agencies*, *email deliverability tools for agencies*, *agency email marketing platform reviews*, *how to choose email automation for clients*.\n\nTen. Ten long-tails. Not because it was cleverΓÇöbecause the alternative was calling it *The System* or *The Pipeline* and he'd already lost enough of his vocabulary to corporate speak.\n\nArthur lived in a GitHub repo, a collection of Python scripts wrapped around GPT-4 with a prompt engineering document that had grown to forty-seven pages. Marcus had written every page. He knew the prompt better than he knew his own middle name.\n\n*You are an expert B2B SaaS reviewer writing for digital agency owners. You have 8+ years experience implementing email automation for clients. You are skeptical of marketing claims. You care about deliverability, segmentation depth, API reliability, and white-label reporting. You write in second person. You include specific pricing scenarios. You never use the phrase \"in today's digital landscape.\"*\n\nHe'd refined that prompt over three weeks. The first version had produced something that read like a LinkedIn thought-leadership post written by a chatbotΓÇöwhich, technically, it was. The second version was better but hallucinated pricing. The third version forgot to mention that ActiveCampaign's agency partner program required a $299/month minimum spend.\n\nVersion forty-seven worked. Mostly.\n\nMarcus opened the terminal. `python arthur.py --keyword \"activecampaign vs klaviyo for agency use\" --format mdx --output content/reviews/activecampaign-vs-klaviyo.mdx`\n\nThe script ran. He opened it.\n\n---\n\n# ActiveCampaign vs Klaviyo: Which Email Automation Wins for Agencies in 2024?\n\n**TL;DR:** ActiveCampaign for complex client journeys. Klaviyo for e-commerce-heavy rosters. Neither is perfect.\n\nIf you're managing email for more than three clients, you've already had the conversation. The one where you explain why Mailchimp's automation builder feels like building a Rube Goldberg machine with duct tape. The one where you justify the budget increase to a founder who thinks \"email is free.\"\n\nI've implemented both platforms across twelve agency clients in the last eighteen months. Here's what the sales reps won't tell you.\n\n---\n\nMarcus read further. The comparison table was accurate. The pricing breakdown included the agency partner program tiers. The \"verdict\" section named specific client archetypes: *The SaaS startup with a 14-day trial sequence*, *The DTC brand doing $2M/year with complex flows*, *The local service business needing review automation*.\n\nIt was good. It was *useful*.\n\nIt also had a paragraph claiming ActiveCampaign's deliverability rate was \"94.2% according to their 2023 transparency report.\"\n\nActiveCampaign had never published a transparency report.\n\nMarcus highlighted the line. Deleted it. Typed: *ActiveCampaign doesn't publish deliverability data. Neither does Klaviyo. Anyone giving you a number is guessing. What I can tell you: both platforms maintain dedicated IP pools for agency partners. Both require domain authentication. Both will throttle you if your clients send cold lists.*\n\nHe saved. `git add . && git commit -m \"Fix hallucinated deliverability stat in AC vs Klaviyo\" && git push`\n\n1:02 AM.\n\nFour more reviews. One pillar page.\n\nHe ran the script four more times. Each iteration cost between $0.41 and $0.53. Each output needed surgeryΓÇöhallucinated features, outdated pricing, a weird tendency to describe Customer.io as \"developer-first\" without explaining what that meant for an agency managing non-technical clients.\n\nBy 2:47 AM, the content folder held six MDX files. Clean frontmatter. Proper slugs. Affiliate link placeholders in the format `{{AFFILIATE:activecampaign}}` that the build script would swap for PartnerStack tracking URLs.\n\n*Deploy to Vercel with Next.js + MDX template.*\n\nThe template existed. He'd built it over two weekends in November, stealing components from shadcn/ui and a Tailwind typography plugin. Dark mode. Table of contents sidebar. Automatic JSON-LD schema for review stars. A sticky CTA bar that appeared after 60% scroll depth.\n\n`npm run build`\n\nThe terminal filled with TypeScript compilation, MDX processing, static generation. Fast. Clean. Each form a confession: *I have no traffic. I have no track record. I have a site I built in my apartment at 3 AM.*\n\nHe got the auto-replies instantly. *Thanks for applying. Our team reviews applications within 5-7 business days.*\n\nFive to seven business days. TIMESTAMPTZ,\n    published_at TIMESTAMPTZ,\n    platform_video_ids JSONB, -- {\"youtube\": \"vid123\", \"tiktok\": \"vid456\"}\n    error_message TEXT,\n    retry_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,\n pod_collection_id VARCHAR(100),\n    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'planned' \n        CHECK (status IN ('planned', 'generating', 'ready', 'published', 'skipped')),\n    video_job_id UUID REFERENCES video_jobs(id),\n    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()\n);\n\n-- Analytics / CTR tracking\nCREATE TABLE video_analytics (\n    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),\n    video_job_id UUID REFERENCES video_jobs(id),\n    platform VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,\n    platform_video_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,\n    date DATE NOT NULL,\n    views BIGINT DEFAULT 0,\n    likes BIGINT DEFAULT 0,\n thumbnail_variant CHAR(1),\n    fetched_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),\n    UNIQUE(video_job_id, platform, date)\n);\n\n-- A/B Test results\nCREATE TABLE ab_tests (\n    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),\n    video_job_id UUID REFERENCES video_jobs(id),\n    variant_a_thumbnail VARCHAR(500),\n    variant_b_thumbnail VARCHAR(500),\n    variant_a_ctr DECIMAL(5,4),\n    variant_b_ctr concluded_at TIMESTAMPTZ,\n    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'running' CHECK (status IN ('running', 'concluded', 'cancelled'))\n);\n\n-- Revenue tracking (all sources -> PayPal)\nCREATE TABLE revenue_events (\n    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),\n    source VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL \n        CHECK (source IN ('youtube_adsense', 'shopify', 'gumroad', 'impact_affiliate', DECIMAL(10,2) DEFAULT 0,\n    net_usd DECIMAL(10,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (amount_usd - fee_usd) STORED,\n    paypal_transaction_id VARCHAR(100),\n    settled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,\n    occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,\n    raw_payload JSONB,\n    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()\n);\n\n-- PayPal payout reconciliation\nCREATE TABLE payout_reconciliation (\n    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT \n  \n  return {\n    lineItems: [\n      ...lineItems,\n      {\n        variant_id: stickerVariantId,\n        quantity: packagingItem.quantity,\n        retail_price: '0.00', // internal cost only\n        name: 'Premium Packaging Sticker'\n      }\n    ]\n  };\n}\n```\n\n### 3.3 Flow 3: Daily Reconciliation (Optional but Recommended)\n**Trigger:** Scheduled daily at 6 AM  \n**Action:** Run **ΓÇ£Order Risk AnalysisΓÇ¥** ΓåÆ Flag orders where:\n- Upsell accepted but Printful status Γëá `fulfilled` after 48h\n- Revenue mismatch: Shopify order total Γëá Printful order total\n\n---\n\n## ≡ƒÆ░ Phase 4: Financial Plumbing (Stripe ΓåÆ PayPal)\n\n| Step | Action | Verification |\n|------|--------|--------------|\n| 1 | Shopify Admin ΓåÆ **Settings ΓåÆ Payments** ΓåÆ Manage Shopify Payments | Confirm **ΓÇ£Daily payoutsΓÇ¥** selected |\n| 2 | Shopify Payments ΓåÆ **Payout schedule** ΓåÆ **Every business day** | Test: Place $1 order ΓåÆ Confirm payout appears in Stripe Dashboard next day |\n| 3 | Stripe Dashboard ΓåÆ **Settings ΓåÆ Payouts** ΓåÆ **Add external account** ΓåÆ Link PayPal Business | Use PayPalΓÇÖs **ΓÇ£Instant TransferΓÇ¥** eligible debit card for same-day access |\n| 4 | **Accounting sync:** Connect **A2X** or **Link My Books** ΓåÆ Auto-post Shopify payouts to QuickBooks/Xero | Reconcile weekly: Shopify Gross Sales = Stripe Net Payouts + Fees |\n\n> ΓÜá∩╕Å **Gotcha:** Printful charges your *Printful Wallet* (not Shopify). environment) and validate with Pydantic.\"\"\"\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Dict, Any\n\nimport yaml\nfrom pydantic import BaseSettings, Field, validator\nfrom pydantic.env_settings import SettingsConfigDict\n\n\nclass RedbubbleConfig(BaseSettings):\n    api_key: str = Field(..., env=\"REDBUBBLE_API_KEY\")\n    user_id: str = Field(..., env=\"REDBUBBLE_USER_ID\")\n    base_url: str = \"https://api.redbubble.com\"\n    upload_url: str = Field(\"${base_url}/v1/items\", env=\"REDBUBBLE_UPLOAD_URL\")\n\n    @validator(\"upload_url\", pre=True)\n    def fill_placeholder(cls, v, values):\n        if isinstance(v, str) and \"${base_url}\" in v:\n            base = values.get(\"base_url\", \"\")\n            return v.replace(\"${base_url}\", base)\n        return v\n\n\nclass TeepublicConfig(BaseSettings):\n    api_key: str = Field(..., env=\"TEEPUBLIC_API_KEY\")\n    user_id: str = Field(..., env=\"TEEPUBLIC_USER_ID\")\n    base_url: str = \"https://api.teepublic.com\"\n    upload_url: str = Field(\"${base_url}/v1/products\", env=\"TEEPUBLIC_UPLOAD_URL\")\n\n    @validator(\"upload_url\", pre=True)\n    def fill_placeholder(cls, v, values):\n        if isinstance(v, str) and \"${base_url}\" in v:\n            base = values.get(\"base_url\", \"\")\n            return v.replace(\"${base_url}\", base)\n        return v\n\n\nclass NicheConfig(BaseSettings):\n    name: str\n    keywords: list[str]\n    default_tags: list[str]\n\n\nclass PriceOptimizationConfig(BaseSettings):\n    redbubble: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n    teepublic: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n    keyword_premium: Dict[str, list[str]] = {}\n\n\nclass Settings(BaseSettings):\n    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_prefix=\"POD_\")\n\n    log_level: str = \"INFO\"\n    batch_size: int = 50\n    retry_attempts: int = 3\n    retry_delay_sec: int = 5\n\n    redbubble: RedbubbleConfig = RedbubbleConfig()\n    teepublic: TeepublicConfig = TeepublicConfig()\n    niches: list[NicheConfig] = []\n    price_optimization: PriceOptimizationConfig = PriceOptimizationConfig()\n    schedule: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n    ai: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n\n    @validator(\"niches\", pre=True)\n    def parse_niches(cls, v, values, **kwargs):\n        # v can be raw YAML list of dicts ΓÇô Pydantic will coerce automatically\n        return v\n\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Public API\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef load_settings(path: Path | str = \"config/settings.yaml\") -> Settings:\n    \"\"\"\n    Load settings from a YAML file, then merge with environment variables.\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    path: Path | str\n        Path to the settings YAML file.\n\n    Returns\n    -------\n    Settings\n        Validated configuration object.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Load YAML\n    with open(path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        raw = yaml.safe_load(f)\n\n    # Merge with environment variables (Pydantic will automatically read env vars)\n    # We'll create a dict of env vars that start with POD_ (as defined in BaseSettings)\n    # and update raw with them ΓÇô this lets us keep everything in one file while still\n    # allowing secrets to be supplied via environment.\n    env_vars = {\n        k: v\n        for k, v in os.environ.items()\n        if k.startswith(\"POD_\") and not k.startswith(\"POD_ENGINE_\")\n    }\n\n    # Apply env vars to raw dict (nested keys are flattened for simplicity)\n    # For a production system you might want a more sophisticated merging strategy.\n    for key, value in env_vars.items():\n        # e.g., POD_REDBUBBLE_API_KEY -> redbubble.api_key\n        parts = key.lower().split(\"_\")\n        target = raw\n        for part in parts[1:]:  # skip 'pod'\n            if isinstance(target, dict) and part in target:\n                target = target[part]\n            else:\n                target = {}\n                break\n        if isinstance(target, dict):\n            # This is a very naive flattening ΓÇô works for the simple schema above.\n            # A real solution would use a deep merge library.\n            pass  # skip for now ΓÇô rely on Pydantic's env prefix.\n\n    # Let Pydantic do the heavy lifting\n    return Settings(**raw)\n\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Example usage\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    cfg = load_settings()\n    print(cfg.json(indent=2))\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6∩╕ÅΓâú `src/design_generator.py`\n\n```python\n# src/design_generator.py\n\"\"\"AIΓÇæbased design generator.\n   Currently uses OpenAI GPTΓÇæ4 Vision (or a mock if no key is provided).\n   Generates a PNG image (base64) that can be uploaded to POD platforms.\n\"\"\"\nimport base64\nimport io\nimport json\nimport logging\nfrom typing import List, Tuple\n\nimport openai\nimport requests\nfrom PIL import Image\nfrom tenacity import retry, stop_after_attempt, wait_fixed\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass DesignGenerator:\n    \"\"\"\n    Generates designs for a given niche using an AI model.\n\n    The generator creates a prompt from the niche keywords, calls the AI API,\n    and returns a PIL Image (RGB) and an associated metadata dict (tags,\n    description, etc.).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config: dict):\n        self.provider = config.get(\"provider\", \"mock\")\n        self.model = config.get(\"model\", \"gpt-4-vision-preview\")\n        self.api_key = config.get(\"api_key\")\n        self.max_tokens = config.get(\"max_tokens_per_prompt\", 1000)\n        self.temperature = config.get(\"temperature\", 0.7)\n\n        if self.provider == \"openai\" and self.api_key:\n            openai.api_key = self.api_key\n            self.client = openai.OpenAI()\n        else:\n            self.client = None\n            logger.warning(\"AI provider not configured ΓÇô using mock designs.\")\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Public API\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def generate_designs(\n        self,\n        niche_name: str,\n        keywords: List[str],\n        count: int = 20,\n    ) -> List[Tuple[Image.Image, dict]]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Generate a batch of designs for a niche.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        niche_name : str\n            HumanΓÇæreadable niche name (e.g., \"Minimalist Quotes\").\n        keywords : list[str]\n            List of keywords to incorporate into the prompt.\n        count : int\n            Number of designs to generate.\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        list[tuple]\n            Each tuple is (PIL.Image, metadata_dict). Metadata includes:\n                - tags (list)\n                - description (str)\n                - prompt (str) ΓÇô useful for debugging\n        \"\"\"\n        designs = []\n        for i in range(count):\n            logger.info(\"Generating design %d/%d for niche '%s'\", i + 1, count, niche_name)\n            image, meta = self._generate_single(niche_name, keywords)\n            designs.append((image, meta))\n        return designs\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Internal helpers\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    @retry(stop=stop_after_attempt(3), wait=wait_fixed(2))\n    def _generate_single(self, niche_name: str, keywords: List[str]) -> Tuple[Image.Image, dict]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Create one design by calling the AI API (or mock) and parse the result.\n        \"\"\"\n        # Build a descriptive prompt\n        prompt = self._build_prompt(niche_name, keywords)\n\n        if self.provider == \"mock\" or not self.client:\n            # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n            # Mock implementation ΓÇô returns a solid colour image with a text label.\n            # In production replace this with a real AI call.\n            # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n            image = self._mock_create_image(prompt)\n            tags = self._derive_tags(keywords)\n            description = self._derive_description(prompt, tags)\n            return image, {\n                \"tags\": tags,\n                \"description\": description,\n                \"prompt\": prompt,\n                \"source\": \"mock\",\n            }\n\n        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n        # Real AI call (OpenAI GPTΓÇæ4 Vision)\n        # ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n        # We need an image prompt; System Prompt\n        if system:\n            self.set_font('Inter', 'B', 9)\n            self.set_text_color(0, 100, 0)\n            self.cell(0, 5, 'System Prompt:', new_x=\"LMARGIN\", new_y=\"NEXT\")\n            self.set_font('JetBrainsMono', '', 8)\n            self.set_text_color(30, 30, 30)\n            self.multi_cell(0, 4, system, border=1, fill=True, new_x=\"LMARGIN\", new_y=\"NEXT\")\n            self.ln(2)\n\n        # User Prompt\n        self.set_font('Inter', 'B', 9)\n        self.set_text_color(0, 0, 150)\n        self.cell(0, 5, 'User Prompt:', new_x=\"LMARGIN\", new_y=\"NEXT\")\n        self.set_font('JetBrainsMono', '', 8)\n        self.set_text_color(30, 30, 30)\n        self.multi_cell(0, 4, prompt, border=1, new_x=\"LMARGIN\", new_y=\"NEXT\")\n        self.ln(4)\n\n# Load JSON, Loop Categories, Add TOC, Output pdf.output(\"AI_Prompt_Pack_v1.0.pdf\")\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. ≡ƒº▒ NOTION TEMPLATE ARCHITECTURE\n**Goal:** High perceived value, filterable database, \"Duplicate to Workspace\" ready.\n\n### A. Architecture (3 Core Workflows)\n\n#### Workflow A: `Content Sync & Build Trigger` (Runs every 15 mins / Webhook)\n1.  **Trigger:** Airtable Trigger (Watch `Tools` table for `Status` = `Published` OR `Last_Synced` updated).\n2.  **Filter:** Group changes by `Site` field.\n3.  **Function Node:** Generate `content.json` payload per site (Tools + Keywords).\n4.  **HTTP Request:** `POST` to **Vercel Deploy Hook** (Specific to each Site App).\n    *   *Vercel Build Process:* Pulls Airtable data via Contentlayer (build time) OR uses ISR `revalidate` (runtime). **Recommendation:** **ISR (Revalidate: 3600s) + On-Demand Revalidation via Webhook.** Faster iteration, no rebuild wait.\n\n#### Workflow B: `On-Demand Revalidation` (Called by Airtable Automation / Manual Button)\n1.  **Trigger:** Webhook (receives `site`, `slug`, `type`).\n2.  **HTTP Request:** `POST` to `https://[site].vercel.app/api/revalidate?secret=[TOKEN]&path=[path]`.\n3.  **Slack/Discord Notification:** \"Page [slug] revalidated on [site]\".\n\n#### Workflow C: `GSC Indexing & Monitoring` (Daily Cron 06:00 UTC)\n1.  **Trigger:** Cron.\n2.  **Loop over 5 Sites:**\n    *   **HTTP Request:** Fetch `sitemap.xml` from `https://[site]/sitemap.xml`.\n    *   **Parse XML:** Extract all `<url><loc>`.\n    *   **Google Indexing API (Batch):** Submit `URL_UPDATED` for new/updated URLs (Limit 200/day/site).\n    *   **GSC Search Analytics API:** Fetch `Clicks`, `Impressions`, `Position` for last 28d.\n    *   **Store:** Write metrics to **Airtable `Performance` Table** or **Postgres (Supabase)** for dashboarding.\n    *   **Alerting:** If `Coverage Error` > 0 or `Indexed Pages` drop > 10% -> Slack Alert.\n\n---\n\n## 5. 1. Load from YAML file (development)\n        if secrets_path is None:\n            secrets_path = os.getenv(\"SECRETS_PATH\", \"config/secrets.yaml\")\n        \n        path = Path(secrets_path)\n        if path.exists():\n            with open(path) as f:\n                self._secrets = yaml.safe_load(f) or {}\n            logger.info(f\"Loaded secrets from {secrets_path}\")\n        else:\n            logger.warning(f\"Secrets file not found: {secrets_path}\")\n        \n        # 2. Override with environment variables (production)\n        self._load_from_env(env_prefix)\n        \n        # 3. Validate required secrets\n        self._validate_required()\n        \n        self._loaded = True\n    \n    def _load_from_env(self, prefix: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Load secrets from environment variables with prefix.\"\"\"\n        env_mapping = {\n            f\"{prefix}SHOPIFY_STORE_DOMAIN\": \"shopify.store_domain\",\n            f\"{prefix}SHOPIFY_ADMIN_API_TOKEN\": \"shopify.admin_api_token\",\n            f\"{prefix}SHOPIFY_WEBHOOK_SECRET\": \"shopify.webhook_secret\",\n            f\"{prefix}PRINTFUL_API_KEY\": \"printful.api_key\",\n            f\"{prefix}PRINTFUL_STORE_ID\": \"printful.store_id\",\n            f\"{prefix}META_APP_ID\": \"meta.app_id\",\n            f\"{prefix}META_APP_SECRET\": \"meta.app_secret\",\n            f\"{prefix}META_ACCESS_TOKEN\": \"meta.access_token\",\n            f\"{prefix}META_BUSINESS_MANAGER_ID\": \"meta.business_manager_id\",\n            f\"{prefix}META_AD_ACCOUNT_EU\": \"meta.ad_accounts.EU\",\n            f\"{prefix}META_AD_ACCOUNT_UK\": \"meta.ad_accounts.UK\",\n            f\"{prefix}META_AD_ACCOUNT_CA\": \"meta.ad_accounts.CA\",\n            f\"{prefix}META_PIXEL_EU\": \"meta.pixels.EU\",\n            f\"{prefix}META_PIXEL_UK\": \"meta.pixels.UK\",\n            f\"{prefix}META_PIXEL_CA\": \"meta.pixels.CA\",\n            f\"{prefix}META_CATALOG_ID\": \"meta.catalog_id\",\n            f\"{prefix}PAYPAL_CLIENT_ID\": \"paypal.client_id\",\n            f\"{prefix}PAYPAL_CLIENT_SECRET\": \"paypal.client_secret\",\n            f\"{prefix}PAYPAL_WEBHOOK_ID\": \"paypal.webhook_id\",\n            f\"{prefix}SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL\": \"monitoring.slack_webhook_url\",\n            f\"{prefix}ALERT_EMAIL\": \"monitoring.alert_email\",\n            f\"{prefix}DATABASE_URL\": \"database.url\",\n            f\"{prefix}REDIS_URL\": \"redis.url\",\n        }\n        \n        for env_var, secret_path in env_mapping.items():\n            value = os.getenv(env_var)\n            if value:\n                self._set_nested(secret_path, value)\n    \n    def _set_nested(self, path: str, value: Any) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Set nested dictionary value using dot notation.\"\"\"\n        keys = path.split('.')\n        current = self._secrets\n        for key in keys[:-1]:\n            if key not in current:\n                current[key] = {}\n            current = current[key]\n        current[keys[-1]] = value\n    \n    def _validate_required(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Validate all required secrets are present.\"\"\"\n        required = [\n            \"shopify.store_domain\",\n            \"shopify.admin_api_token\",\n            \"shopify.webhook_secret\",\n            \"printful.api_key\",\n            \"printful.store_id\",\n            \"meta.app_id\",\n            \"meta.app_secret\",\n            \"meta.access_token\",\n            \"meta.business_manager_id\",\n        ]\n        \n        missing = []\n        for path in required:\n            if not self.get(path):\n                missing.append(path)\n        \n        if missing:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Missing required secrets: {missing}\")\n    \n    def get(self, path: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:\n        \"\"\"Get secret value using dot notation.\"\"\"\n        keys = path.split('.')\n        current = self._secrets\n        for key in keys:\n            if isinstance(current, dict) and key in current:\n                current = current[key]\n            else:\n                return default\n        return current\n    \n    def get_region","result_files":"","created_at":"2026-08-05 23:10:28","updated_at":"2026-08-05 ≡ƒ¢á PHASE 1: DATA EXTRACTION & ANALYSIS (The \"Brain\")\n\n### 1.1 Prerequisites & Secrets Management\n**Do not hardcode credentials.** Use `.env` or GitHub Secrets / Vercel Env Vars.\n\n```bash\n# .env required variables\nSHOPIFY_STORE=your-store.myshopify.com\nSHOPIFY_ADMIN_TOKEN=shpat_xxxxxxxxxxxxx (Admin API Access Token: read_orders, read_products)\nPRINTFUL_API_KEY=xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx\nMETA_ACCESS_TOKEN=EAAxxxxxxxxxxxx (System User Token: ads_management, granted)\nTIKTOK_ADVERTISER_ID=xxxxxxxxxxxx\nPAYPAL_EMAIL=finance@yourdomain.com\n```\n\n### 1.2 Python Script: `find_winners.py`\n*Pulls 90 days orders, aggregates by Design (Variant Title/Metafield), outputs Top 3.*\n\n```python\nimport os, requests, json, pandas as pd\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\nfrom dotenv import load_dotenv\n\nload_dotenv()\n\nSHOP = os.getenv(\"SHOPIFY_STORE\")\nTOKEN = os.getenv(\"SHOPIFY_ADMIN_TOKEN\")\nHEADERS = {\"X-Shopify-Access-Token\": TOKEN, \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\"}\nGRAPHQL_URL = f\"https://{SHOP}/admin/api/2024-01/graphql.json\"\n\n# 1. FETCH ORDERS (Last 90 Days)\nninety_days_ago = (datetime.now() - timedelta(days=90)).isoformat()\n\nquery = \"\"\"\nquery($query: String!, $first: Int!, $after: String) {\n  orders(query: $query, first: $first, after: $after, sortKey: PROCESSED_AT) {\n    pageInfo { hasNextPage, endCursor }\n    edges {\n      node {\n        id\n        totalPriceSet { shopMoney { amount, currencyCode } }\n        lineItems(first: 50) {\n          edges {\n            node {\n              title\n              variantTitle\n              quantity\n              originalUnitPriceSet { shopMoney { amount } }\n              # CRITICAL: Identify the Design. \n              # Option A: Variant Title (e.g., \"Design: Skull Rose / Color: Black / Size: M\")\n              # Option B: Custom Metafield (namespace: \"pod\", key: \"design_id\")\n              product { metafield(namespace: \"pod\", key: \"design_id\") { value } }\n            }\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n\"\"\"\n\ndef fetch_all_orders():\n    all_orders = []\n    has_next = True\n    cursor = None\n    while has_next:\n        vars = {\"query\": f\"processed_at:>={ninety_days_ago} financial_status:paid\", \"first\": 250, \"after\": cursor}\n        resp = requests.post(GRAPHQL_URL, headers=HEADERS, json={\"query\": query, \"variables\": vars})\n        data = resp.json()[\"data\"][\"orders\"]\n        all_orders.extend([e[\"node\"] for e in data[\"edges\"]])\n        has_next = data[\"pageInfo\"][\"hasNextPage\"]\n        cursor = data[\"pageInfo\"][\"endCursor\"]\n    return all_orders\n\n# 2. AGGREGATE REVENUE BY DESIGN\ndef analyze(orders):\n    rows = []\n    for o in orders:\n        for li in o[\"lineItems\"][\"edges\"]:\n            n = li[\"node\"]\n            # Extract Design ID - ADAPT THIS LOGIC TO YOUR STORE STRUCTURE\n            design_id = n[\"product\"][\"metafield\"][\"value\"] if n[\"product\"].get(\"metafield\") else n[\"variantTitle\"].split(\"/\")[0].replace(\"Design: \", \"\").strip()\n            revenue = float(n[\"originalUnitPriceSet\"][\"shopMoney\"][\"amount\"]) * n[\"quantity\"]\n            rows.append({\"design_id\": design_id, \"revenue\": revenue, \"units\": n[\"quantity\"], \"title\": n[\"title\"]})\n    \n    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)\n    agg = df.groupby(\"design_id\").agg(total_revenue=(\"revenue\", \"sum\"), total_units=(\"units\", \"sum\"), sample_title=(\"title\", \"first\")).reset_index()\n    top3 = agg.nlargest(3, \"total_revenue\")\n    return top3.to_dict(orient=\"records\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    print(\"Fetching orders...\")\n    orders = fetch_all_orders()\n    print(f\"Analyzing {len(orders)} orders...\")\n    winners = analyze(orders)\n    print(json.dumps(winners, indent=2))\n    \n    # Save for Phase 2\n    with open(\"winners.json\", \"w\") as f: json.dump(winners, f)\n```\n\n**Output (`winners.json`):**\n```json\n[\n  {\"design_id\": \"skull-rose-01\", \"total_revenue\": 4250.00, \"total_units\": 85, \"sample_title\": \"Skull Rose Tee\"},\n  {\"design_id\":
